{"forms":[{"form":"plunders","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"plundering","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"plundered","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"plundered","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The mercenaries plundered the small town.","type":"example"},{"text":"The shopkeeper was plundered of his possessions by the burglar.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To pillage, take or destroy all the goods of, by force (as in war); to raid, sack."],"synonyms":[{"word":"ravage"},{"word":"rifle"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The mercenaries plundered all the goods they found.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To take (goods) by pillage."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"“Now to plunder, mateys!” screamed a buccaneer, to cries of “Arrgh!” and “Aye!” all around.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To take by force or wrongfully; to commit robbery or looting, to raid."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The miners plundered the jungle for its diamonds till it became a muddy waste.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To make extensive (over)use of, as if by plundering; to use or use up wrongfully."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To take unexpectedly."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To amass ill-gotten wealth (by public officials) through a combination or series of overt criminal acts."],"qualifier":"criminal law","tags":["Philippines"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈplʌndə/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"enpr":"plŭn'də","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈplʌndɚ/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˈplɜn-/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":252399,"upstream_offset":1252882942,"upstream_record_sha256":"6bb7de633995c01bfb1de073f71919f5f0a2d2d0580cfd6a2cebef0869eb48dd","word":"plunder"}
